##[start-action display=Run : parse toolchain version;id=__dtolnay_rust-toolchain.parse]
##[group]Run : parse toolchain version
: parse toolchain version
if [[ -z $toolchain ]]; then
# GitHub does not enforce `required: true` inputs itself. https://github.com/actions/runner/issues/1070
echo "'toolchain' is a required input" >&2
exit 1
elif [[ $toolchain =~ ^stable' '[0-9]+' '(year|month|week|day)s?' 'ago$ ]]; then
if [[ Linux == macOS ]]; then
echo "toolchain=1.$((($(date -v-$(sed 's/stable \([0-9]*\) \(.\).*/\1\2/' <<< $toolchain) +%s)/60/60/24-16569)/7/6))" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
else
echo "toolchain=1.$((($(date --date "${toolchain#stable }" +%s)/60/60/24-16569)/7/6))" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
fi
elif [[ $toolchain =~ ^stable' 'minus' '[0-9]+' 'releases?$ ]]; then
echo "toolchain=1.$((($(date +%s)/60/60/24-16569)/7/6-${toolchain//[^0-9]/}))" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
elif [[ $toolchain =~ ^1\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
echo "toolchain=1.$((i=${toolchain#1.}, c=($(date +%s)/60/60/24-16569)/7/6, i+9*i*(10*i<=c)+90*i*(100*i<=c)))" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
else
echo "toolchain=$toolchain" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
fi

MSRV_FEATURES: --no-default-features --features minimal-application,bugreport,build-assets

toolchain: stable

1 │ use crate::line_range::{HighlightedLineRanges, LineRanges};
2 │ use crate::nonprintable_notation::{BinaryBehavior, NonprintableNotation};
3 │ #[cfg(feature = "paging")]
4 │ use crate::paging::PagingMode;

9 │ 
10 │ #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
11 │ pub enum VisibleLines {
12 │     /// Show all lines which are included in the line ranges
13 │     Ranges(LineRanges),
14 │ 
15 │     #[cfg(feature = "git")]
16 │     /// Only show lines surrounding added/deleted/modified lines
17 │     DiffContext(usize),
18 │ }
19 │ 
20 │ impl VisibleLines {
21 │     pub fn diff_mode(&self) -> bool {
22 │         match self {
23 │             Self::Ranges(_) => false,
24 │             #[cfg(feature = "git")]
25 │             Self::DiffContext(_) => true,
26 │         }
27 │     }
28 │ }
29 │ 
30 │ impl Default for VisibleLines {
31 │     fn default() -> Self {
32 │         VisibleLines::Ranges(LineRanges::default())
33 │     }
34 │ }
35 │ 
36 │ #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
37 │ pub struct Config<'a> {
38 │     /// The explicitly configured language, if any
39 │     pub language: Option<&'a str>,
40 │ 
41 │     /// The fallback syntax used when auto-detection fails
42 │     pub fallback_syntax: Option<&'a str>,
43 │ 
44 │     /// Whether or not to show/replace non-printable characters like space, tab and newline.
45 │     pub show_nonprintable: bool,
46 │ 
47 │     /// The configured notation for non-printable characters
48 │     pub nonprintable_notation: NonprintableNotation,
49 │ 
50 │     /// How to treat binary content
51 │     pub binary: BinaryBehavior,
52 │ 
53 │     /// The character width of the terminal
54 │     pub term_width: usize,
55 │ 
56 │     /// The width of tab characters.
57 │     /// Currently, a value of 0 will cause tabs to be passed through without expanding them.
58 │     pub tab_width: usize,
59 │ 
60 │     /// Whether or not to simply loop through all input (`cat` mode)
61 │     pub loop_through: bool,
62 │ 
63 │     /// Whether or not the output should be colorized
64 │     pub colored_output: bool,
65 │ 
66 │     /// Whether or not the output terminal supports true color
67 │     pub true_color: bool,
68 │ 
69 │     /// Style elements (grid, line numbers, ...)
70 │     pub style_components: StyleComponents,
71 │ 
72 │     /// If and how text should be wrapped
73 │     pub wrapping_mode: WrappingMode,
74 │ 
75 │     /// Pager or STDOUT
76 │     #[cfg(feature = "paging")]
77 │     pub paging_mode: PagingMode,
78 │ 
79 │     /// Specifies which lines should be printed
80 │     pub visible_lines: VisibleLines,
81 │ 
82 │     /// The syntax highlighting theme
83 │     pub theme: String,
84 │ 
85 │     /// File extension/name mappings
86 │     pub syntax_mapping: SyntaxMapping<'a>,
87 │ 
88 │     /// Command to start the pager
89 │     pub pager: Option<&'a str>,
90 │ 
91 │     /// Whether or not to use ANSI italics
92 │     pub use_italic_text: bool,
93 │ 
94 │     /// Ranges of lines which should be highlighted with a special background color
95 │     pub highlighted_lines: HighlightedLineRanges,
96 │ 
97 │     /// Whether or not to allow custom assets. If this is false or if custom assets (a.k.a.
98 │     /// cached assets) are not available, assets from the binary will be used instead.
99 │     pub use_custom_assets: bool,
100 │ 
101 │     // Whether or not to use $LESSOPEN if set
102 │     #[cfg(feature = "lessopen")]
103 │     pub use_lessopen: bool,
104 │ 
105 │     // Whether or not to set terminal title when using a pager
106 │     pub set_terminal_title: bool,
107 │ 
108 │     /// The maximum number of consecutive empty lines to display
109 │     pub squeeze_lines: Option<usize>,
110 │ 
111 │     // Whether or not to strip ANSI escape codes from the input
112 │     pub strip_ansi: StripAnsiMode,
113 │ 
114 │     // Substitute terminal-active and spoofing-relevant bytes; implies strip_ansi.
115 │     pub sanitize: StripAnsiMode,
116 │ 
117 │     /// Whether or not to produce no output when input is empty
118 │     pub quiet_empty: bool,
119 │ 
120 │     /// Whether or not to use unbuffered input reading for streaming use cases
121 │     pub unbuffered: bool,
122 │ }
123 │ 
124 │ #[cfg(all(feature = "minimal-application", feature = "paging"))]
125 │ pub fn get_pager_executable(config_pager: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
126 │     crate::pager::get_pager(config_pager)
127 │         .ok()
128 │         .flatten()
129 │         .and_then(|pager| {
130 │             if pager.kind != crate::pager::PagerKind::Builtin {
131 │                 Some(pager.bin)
132 │             } else {
133 │                 None
134 │             }
135 │         })
136 │ }
137 │ 
138 │ #[test]
139 │ fn default_config_should_include_all_lines() {
140 │     use crate::line_range::MaxBufferedLineNumber;
141 │     use crate::line_range::RangeCheckResult;
142 │ 
143 │     assert_eq!(
144 │         LineRanges::default().check(17, MaxBufferedLineNumber::Tentative(17)),
145 │         RangeCheckResult::InRange
146 │     );
147 │ }

152 │     use crate::line_range::RangeCheckResult;
153 │ 
154 │     assert_ne!(
155 │         Config::default()
156 │             .highlighted_lines
157 │             .0
158 │             .check(17, MaxBufferedLineNumber::Tentative(17)),
159 │         RangeCheckResult::InRange
160 │     );
161 │ }
